2026.11.12 早报

覆盖窗口：2026-11-11T06:00:00+08:00 → 2026-11-12T06:00:00+08:00 · 更新时间：2026.11.12 06:26 北京时间 · 354 条

窗口：2026-11-11 06:00 至 2026-11-12 06:00（北京时间，UTC+8）｜监控名单 134 个账号｜所有时间均为北京时间。

Executive Summary｜核心结论

本期窗口为 2026-11-11 06:00 至 2026-11-12 06:00（北京时间，UTC+8）。其中 11 日 06:00 至 21:30 与本日晚报《2026.11.11 - X Digest - 晚报》窗口重叠：Anthropic 卖方会所称不续 SpaceX 高价算力与 5.5 家族训练成本、路演毛利率口径、软银电力设备改计划及已确认亏损、台积电股息与 409.3 亿美元资本拨款及管理层“需求未见放缓”、存储长协与移动 DRAM 涨价、KLA/Onto 毛利被侵蚀、CoreWeave 预付款/涨价与 Altman 关于需求重复计算的表态，以及中国数据中心造价等，已在晚报出现。本期仍完整收录该时段以保持 24 小时覆盖；增量主线在 11 日 21:30 至 12 日 06:00（约对应美东周二白天至夜间，Veterans Day 美股开市）：（1）Google 正式推出 Gemini Decisions API / Decision 1，官方给出极低输入价与输出免费，但第三方 Decision Index 显示它并非该任务前沿，且按任务成本并不便宜；（2）TPU 8i Max 被确认改为每芯片 384GB、8 层 HBM，并宣称带宽翻倍以加强推理，同时仍有“TPU 不够用”和推理商因拿不到配额而自购芯片的市场读法；（3）彭博被转述称 Anthropic IPO 与私募合计最多筹资约 904 亿美元、完全稀释估值约 1.4 万亿美元且已多倍超购，同时出现用公司 run rate 反驳信用卡面板“增速失速”的争论；（4）一线使用侧称持久代理已占其 AI 支出约 45%，产品跟进速度开始被用来解释份额，而不是只看模型名。以下“分析/含义”为本摘要的综合判断；凡标注“据报/传闻/作者观点/卖方或媒体转述”的内容均来自 X 帖子或其转述，未经独立核实，数字均以原帖为准。

一、Gemini Decisions API：官方把“选择”做成低价产品，评测显示它还不是该任务的前沿

事实层：@sundarpichai 宣布推出 Gemini Decisions API，由新模型 Gemini Decision 1 驱动，在 Google AI Studio 提供。它不做逐 token 文本生成，而是封闭集合决策：单次最多评分 1,000 道题，输入可含文本、图像、音频、视频和 PDF；三种类型为是/否、多项选择（最多 255 项）和 rubric 打分。官方标价为每百万输入 token 0.10 美元，输出 token 计费为 0 美元，并称约比 Flash-Lite 便宜 5 倍；同时称在若干评测上领先且校准误差最低。Google 内部团队已在使用。@OpenRouter 称 Decision 1 首日上架，口径与官方定价一致。

第三方评测（非官方审计）：@ArtificialAnlys 给出 Artificial Analysis Decision Index v2.1 得分 66，与 Elephant 9B、Jev 1.14 相当，落后领先的 Drex Max v1.1 与 GLiDE v1.2（均为 73 分）7 分；按该指数，Decision 1 每百万任务成本约 345 美元，约为 Drex Max（103 美元）的三倍，作者将其归因于更高定价。知识与推理类基准领先，但并非 Decision Index 的前沿。@kimmonismus 另转述 Google 自测：在 7 项共有基准中的 5 项优于 Jev 1.14，但 Jev 输入价约 0.042 美元/百万 token，仍低于 Decision 1 的 0.10 美元。同窗口还有 @AndrewCurran_ 的产品传闻：Gemini 4 Flash（Hydrogen）“似乎即将发布”，并称 Carbon 已在 Google 搜索 AI Mode 上线——这是作者观察，不是官方发布说明。

分析/含义：Google 是在把“判断/选择”从通用生成里拆出来，用输出免费、输入极低的标价去切 Flash 级和专用决策模型的工作负载，并立刻经 OpenRouter 分发。但不要把官方“若干评测领先”写成全面领先：独立 Decision Index 显示它还落后头部约 7 分，而且“输入便宜、输出免费”并不等于任务总成本更低。对 GOOGL，这是产品与定价实验；对专用决策模型创业公司，是新的价格锚，但还不是性能锚。AlphaGenome 2（@demishassabis / @sundarpichai）同日发布，针对不同细胞类型的 DNA 变异影响，目前面向学术/非商业 API，近端云收入含义弱于 Decisions API。

二、TPU 8i Max 的新规格是 HBM 层数与带宽；“不够用”和自购芯片仍是市场读法

规格层（@SemiAnalysis_）：Google 公布 TPU 8i Max（又称 Whalefish）。公告确认每芯片 384GB HBM，并改为 8 层（8-hi）HBM，而原版 TPU 8i 为 12 层；作者称数月前已向 Accelerator Model 订阅者报告过这一方向。帖文并指出，这与 Nvidia 下一代 Rubin Ultra 转向 8-hi HBM 的做法一致。Google 宣传推理性能明显更强，因为每个封装的 HBM 带宽变为原来的两倍；作者强调，对推理性能而言，带宽比容量更重要。TPU 8i Max 本身在昨日摘要中已出现，本期增量是 384GB、8-hi 对比 12-hi，以及“带宽翻倍”这一推理向卖点。

需求与替代（均为观点或转述，不是 Google/Broadcom 官方订单）：@SouthernValue95 引用一段讨论，称“今天最好的架构是 Google TPU，因为它被当成单一系统来设计”，NVIDIA 风格架构在向这个方向靠拢，并出现“TPU 非常成功，但不够用”的判断。另有一条回复称“They’ve ordered 15GW of TPU from AVGO/GOOG”，成本“不到 1/5”——“They”在该条中并未写清，不得写成已核实的 15GW 订单。关键词扫描中 @0xkydo 给出一组未注明来源、且原文在句末被截断的芯片结构数字：称 Anthropic 约 268 万颗芯片里英伟达只有约 8 万颗（约 3%），其余为 Trainium/TPU，而 OpenAI 约 88% 为英伟达、xAI 与微软为 100%。这与“TPU/Trainium 替代”叙事相符，但作者未给出数据来源，不能当成公司披露。@steph_palazzolo 转述 The Information：推理提供商因拿不到配额，越来越多考虑自购芯片并自己运营数据中心，同时也更多地使用一批规模更小、安全并不总是最好的云。

分析/含义：8-hi、384GB 和“带宽翻倍”会改变 HBM 的产品组合（更矮的堆叠、单芯片更大容量、更偏向推理带宽），并与 Rubin Ultra 的 8-hi 路径形成对照，这是对 SK 海力士、美光等供应商结构的具体线索，而不是新的出货指引。‘TPU 不够用 / 将更多外售’和 15GW 仍是市场口头禅；The Information 关于推理商自购芯片的报道，则说明在配额紧张时，买方会绕开传统 GPU 云。不要把规格解读写成已经落地的外售协议。

三、Anthropic 的新数字主要来自媒体转述和作者口径，信用卡面板争议不能单独定调

IPO 筹资（媒体转述，非招股书原文）：@kimmonismus 称据彭博，Anthropic IPO 已多倍超额认购；公司与联合创始人旨在通过 IPO 和私募最多筹资 904 亿美元，完全稀释估值约 1.4 万亿美元。@AndrewCurran_ 亦称正在讨论筹资 900 亿美元、认购“非常踊跃”，并预告周五有 Dario Amodei 直播。@aleabitoreddit 给出相近的 1.4 万亿美元估值和“倍数超购”说法，并把总融资向约 1,000 亿美元引申——后一步是作者发挥，不是彭博原文。关键词扫描中，彭博官方账号 @business 发布报道《OpenAI, Anthropic Valuations Fuel VC Fears of a Startup Correction》，把高估值与创业公司估值回调担忧放到同一条新闻里；@RealVision 称 1.4 万亿美元目标已较夏季约 2 万亿美元的说法低约 30%，并提到 11 月 18 日可能是 70–78 美元定价考验——这是该账号的读法，不是交易所通知。@wallstengine 称盘前某种 pre-IPO 永续合约隐含估值约 1.82 万亿美元（较前上涨约 8%），这是合约隐含价格，不是 IPO 定价。另有未注明来源的快讯称 OpenAI 单季收入已超过 100 亿美元，以及低粉丝账号转述华尔街日报的收入/亏损对照（OpenAI 收入 100 亿、亏损 109 亿 vs Anthropic 收入 170 亿、亏损 5,700 万）——均未附原文链接，不得当成已审计财报。这些数字比晚报里的路演毛利率故事更接近“发行规模与市场情绪”，但仍是二手转述。

收入节奏之争：@benitoz 称 Anthropic“刚披露”收入 run rate 一个月内从 710 亿美元升至 810 亿美元（约 14%），并以此反驳 Ramp 样本里 token 支出客户仅增长 0.5% 的唱空；他指出该方法论排除订阅。810 亿美元与昨日摘要中出现过的“10 月 ARR 约 810 亿”量级一致，但“一个月从 710 亿到 810 亿、公司刚披露”是作者表述，本期未见原始 filing 摘录。@TechFundies 与 @BenBajarin 则提醒：Ramp 一类信用卡面板不宜单独做准确预测，且通常不覆盖企业合同。@JaredSleeper 用 OpenRouter 公开 API 估算 OAI/Anthropic 支出份额，认为来回切换的波动“前所未见”，并称把它与信用卡数据结合后，更贴近已公布 ARR。

其他必须降权的数字：@zephyr_z9 转述一组“他们”的收入指引——2027 年 1,400 亿美元、2028 年 2,800 亿美元，2026 年 525 亿美元（假设四季度达到 193 亿美元目标），并认为年末 ARR 约 1,000 亿不现实、更可能约 900 亿。这些数字与昨日讨论的 Anthropic 四季度约 193 亿美元指引相邻，但原帖未写明公司名称，本期未核到原始文件，不能当成官方指引写入事实层。@RihardJarc 估计 ANT 开始交易后，亚马逊持有的股份与票据可能占 AMZN 总市值的 13%–16.5%，这是前瞻估算。@tszzl 明确反对卖方会上的“赢家通吃”：若市场真是赢家通吃，就不会有 Anthropic。

分析/含义：增量里仍然没有新的已备案招股条款。能改定价预期的是“最多约 904 亿美元筹资 / 约 1.4 万亿美元完全稀释估值 / 多倍超购”这一彭博口径——它把发行做成一次极大的现金与估值事件，但超购和目标估值都还不是定价结果。Ramp 与 run rate 的对打说明：用卡数据看实验室增速，至少要先分清订阅、合同和 token 消耗。对 AMZN，ANT 上市后的账面与票据敞口可能变得很大，但 13%–16.5% 只是作者区间。继续跟踪周五直播、正式披露和实际定价，而不是把转述数字写成已完成的融资。

四、使用侧：持久代理开始占支出，产品跟进速度被用来解释份额

@dylan522p（SemiAnalysis）称，持久代理（Persistent Agents）是下一阶段的用量解锁，其 AI 支出已有 45% 用于持久代理；他认为 Claude Tags 与 OpenAI 的 Slack agent 体验差，主要使用 Perplexity Computer（多模型），并称 Perplexity 跟进功能很快。补充帖说，他们的用例仍主要跑 Anthropic 模型，但认为 Anthropic 与 OpenAI 的产品力和反馈响应更弱。@AravSrinivas 将“Perplexity Computer”这一名字归到 dylan 早前的博文，并区分 Teammates 与 Computer。@BenBajarin 提供另一条个人用量观察：知识工作中 Claude/Opus/Fable 的月度 token 成本不到 200 美元，OpenAI 模型则明显高于这一水平——这是个人账单，不是企业合同。@OpenRouter 给出平台数据：MAI Transcribe 2 自推出以来请求量增至 12 倍，上周转录 850 万次，高于 9 月首周的 70 万；同期整个语音转写品类请求约 3 倍。GLM 5.3 Flash 用户在过去一个月 TTFT 降 40%、吞吐 +10%、每百万 token 价格降 17%，被归因于新的路由算法。

分析/含义：如果“持久代理”真的在一部分重度用户里占到近一半支出，那么份额竞争会更多发生在编排、记忆、权限和跟进速度上，而不仅仅是下一档基础模型。这与 Decision 1 这类“把一类任务从通用生成里拆走”的产品策略是同一方向。但 45% 和“不到 200 美元”都是单一使用者或单一平台的观察，不能外推成行业结构。OpenRouter 的转写和路由数据更硬一些，说明应用层流量可以在短时间里重新分配，可仍是该平台样本。

五、政策与旁证：对华芯片新限制仍是转述；CPU 份额和存储反弹是次主线

政策：@AndrewCurran_ 称特朗普已批准对输华芯片采取新的打压，并在白宫会议中征求马斯克意见；同时称商务部长 Lutnick 因黄仁勋相关事件面临更多负面报道，其位置“并非不可能”受到影响。另有帖称《经济学人》数小时前呼吁 Lutnick 辞职。这些都是作者转述或配图，不是已公布的法规文本或人事决定，不能写成已经生效的出口管制。企业侧，@AndrewCurran_ 称美联航已禁止个人 AI 助手进入其系统并禁止用于订票——是单个客户的风险与合规动作，不是全行业禁令。

计算与存储旁证：@BenBajarin 根据 Mercury 的 x86 服务器数据外推，AMD 份额从 2024 年三季度 33.8% 升至 2025 年三季度 39.4%、2026 年三季度 45.6%，约每年提升 6 个点；若趋势延续，可能在 2027 年中超过 Intel 的服务器收入、在 2028–29 年超过出货量。这是线性外推，不是公司指引。存储与 AI 基建股在 Veterans Day 开市中大涨，@TradexWhisperer 截图显示美光涨幅超过 13%，@PhotonCap 列举 AXTI、AAOI、Bloom Energy、LITE 等盘中涨幅；关键词扫描里 @pcgamer 报道铠侠把 640 块 SSD 做成单机柜约 157 PB 的存储方案，作为 AI 数据中心容量的产品演示，不是出货或定价指引。@flyingwhale919 根据一张 GPU 租赁价表认为从 A100 到 B300 的租金趋势仍向上，对 neocloud 是好消息。对冲观点来自 @zephyr_z9：很难相信 2027/28 年仍能以极高溢价出租 Ampere/Hopper，除非数据中心建设因监管大幅放缓。@patrick_oshag 用配图比较：Ozempic/Mounjaro 背后两款 GLP-1 药物 2025 年收入 710 亿美元，对照 OpenAI+Anthropic 的 180 亿美元；“今年截至 9 月”则为 700 亿美元对 560 亿美元——用来说明实验室收入在快速追赶，但口径是作者整理，不是经审计对照。中国新能源车数据（@tphuang 转述 CAA 等）显示渗透率继续抬升，同时有渠道压货和整体零售下滑，与本期 AI/算力主线相对独立，完整条目见作者部分。

分析/含义：对投资判断，政策帖需要等正式文本；CPU 份额趋势和存储/光模块的盘中反弹，只能作为对“需求是否已经断裂”的情绪与旁证，不能推翻晚报里已经提出的融资成本、长协锁价和设备商毛利被侵蚀这些更慢的变量。

Highlights / Top 10｜按投资重要性排序

#1 ★@sundarpichai · 2026-11-12 02:15 北京时间

要点：我们今天发布 Gemini Decisions API，由我们的新模型 Gemini Decision 1 驱动，已在 @GoogleAIStudio 上线。

它不是逐 token 生成文本，而是为封闭集选择而设计，可在单次传递中对最多 1,000 个问题打分，覆盖文本、图像、音频、视频和 PDF。几点细节：

- 3 种决策类型：是/否、多项选择（最多 255 个选项）+ rubric scoring（评分规则打分）
- 输入 $0.10 / 1M tokens，输出 tokens 计费为 $0（比 Flash-Lite 便宜约 5 倍）
- 在多项基准评测上领先，校准误差最低

Google 内部团队已经在用它构建，下面是 Stitch Rapid 在数秒内设计完整产品流程的演示（1 倍速）

为何重要：Google 官方发布 Gemini Decisions API / Decision 1：封闭决策、输入 0.10 美元/百万 token、输出免费。这是本期最硬的产品与定价事实，直接关系到判断类工作负载如何从通用生成模型中被拆走。

原帖：https://x.com/sundarpichai/status/2120581155078729728 ｜ 跳至完整条目

#2 @SemiAnalysis_ · 2026-11-12 02:03 北京时间

要点：Google 发布了 TPU 8i Max，又称 Whalefish。一个有意思的细节是：此次宣布确认 8i Max 每芯片将有 384GB HBM，意味着它改用 8-hi HBM，而原来的 TPU 8i 用的是 12-hi HBM——这一点我们几个月前就已向 Accelerator Model 订阅者报告过。这追随了 Nvidia 将下一代 Rubin Ultra 改用 8-hi HBM 的做法。

Google 宣传 TPU 8i Max 的推理性能要强得多，因为每个封装现在拥有两倍的 HBM 带宽。带宽对推理性能的重要性高于容量，我们最近在 4-hi HBM 的文章里解释过，可在此阅读：https://t.co/HBWMhtJzy6

为何重要：SemiAnalysis 确认 TPU 8i Max 每芯片 384GB HBM、由原 12-hi 改为 8-hi，并称封装带宽翻倍、对标 Rubin Ultra 的 8-hi 路径。这是对 HBM 产品组合和推理性能卖点的具体规格，而不是新的出货指引。

原帖：https://x.com/SemiAnalysis_/status/2120578135594791297 ｜ 跳至完整条目

#3 @kimmonismus · 2026-11-11 21:58 北京时间

要点：Anthropic 眼下形势看起来非常好：据彭博社报道，其 IPO 已被多倍超额认购，投资者求购的股份远超可供数量。

该公司及其联合创始人计划通过 IPO 和一次私募配售筹资至多 904 亿美元，目标完全稀释估值约为 1.4 万亿美元。

为何重要：转述彭博：Anthropic IPO 已多倍超购，IPO 加私募最多筹资约 904 亿美元，完全稀释估值约 1.4 万亿美元。这是增量里最完整的发行规模口径，但仍是媒体转述，不是定价或招股书原文。

2026-11-11 11:32 北京时间 ｜ Source: @nodiligence ｜ 原帖链接

$MU $SKHY

存储厂商已经提前卖掉了未来几年中很大一块产量，而股票却被抛得像它们什么都没卖掉一样。

美光距离其历史收盘高点大约下跌了 40%，它有 26 份长期协议，覆盖到 2030 年超过 35% 的营收，背后约有 320 亿美元的客户承诺，其中大部分是现金定金。SK hynix 在首尔的股价较 6 月高点下跌了近 60%，已与 14 家客户签约，锁定其 2027 和 2028 年超过一半的产能。

这些协议大多带有价格下限和上限，而且已经交纳现金定金的客户，是最不可能违约走开的。

市场看起来像是在把未签约那部分的悬崖，当成整个公司来定价。如果 AI 订单下滑，未签约部分会首先、并且很快重新定价，但已签约部分仍会按地板价或更高的价格继续开票。

等到 SK hynix 在 1 月下旬发布四季报时，我们就会知道 DRAM 出货量是否像其指引的那样，实现了中段 20% 左右的增长。

原文：$MU $SKHY

memory makers have sold a big chunk of the next few years in advance, and the stocks are getting dumped like they sold nothing.

micron is about 40% off its record close, and it has 26 long-term deals covering more than 35% of revenue through 2030, backed by about $32bn of customer commitments that are mostly cash deposits. SK hynix's shares in seoul are almost 60% below their june peak, with 14 customers signed for more than half its 2027 and 2028 capacity.

most of those deals come with a floor and a ceiling on price, and a customer who's put down a cash deposit is the last one to walk away.

the market looks like it's pricing the cliff on the part that isn't contracted as if it were the whole company. if AI orders slip, the uncontracted part reprices first and fast, but the contracted part keeps billing at or above its floor.

when SK hynix reports its q4 report in late january, that's when we'll know whether DRAM shipments came in up in the mid-20% range like it guided.

2026-11-11 18:32 北京时间 ｜ Source: @nodiligence ｜ 原帖链接

在美国以外，我这张资金地图上的 269 笔 AI 数据中心融资里，只有 28 笔显示出任何现金。在美国则是 143 笔中的 49 笔，而这 49 笔里有 40 笔是投资者在第一天就付钱的债券或 ABS。

美国看起来更好，主要是因为它有这类资产的债券市场。弗吉尼亚或得州的一座数据中心一旦建成并租出去，就可以再融资成债券或 ABS，一旦做成，现金就变得可见。美国以外，这 269 笔融资里有 151 笔是银行贷款，而银行贷款几乎从不说明已提取了多少。

东南亚是最清楚的例子。马来西亚、泰国和印尼在我的地图上合计有 89 个站点，它们的 39 笔融资没有一笔显示出现金。韩国是 30 笔中的 2 笔，日本 12 笔中的 1 笔，澳大利亚 17 笔中的 1 笔，德国 14 笔中的 1 笔。

今年秋天卡住的那些美国贷款，至少在大楼建成后还有一个债券市场可以去。这里很多项目没有显而易见的这类出口，所以贷款很可能更长时间地留在当初放贷的那家银行手里。

很难知道那里坐着多少建设风险，因为这恰好是交易外部的人看不到的那一部分。但这项建设离美国越远，可见度就越低，其中也就有越多可能最终留在银行资产负债表上，而且没有明显的退出路径。

原文：outside the US, only 28 of the 269 AI data-centre financings in my money map show any cash at all. in the US it’s 49 of 143, and 40 of those 49 are bonds or ABS that investors paid for on day one.

the US looks better mostly because it has a bond market for this stuff. once a data centre in virginia or texas is finished and leased, it can get refinanced into bonds or ABS, and the moment it does, the cash becomes visible. outside the US, 151 of the 269 financings are bank loans, and bank loans almost never say how much was drawn.

southeast asia is the clearest case. malaysia, thailand and indonesia have 89 sites in my map between them, and none of their 39 financings show cash. south korea is 2 of 30, japan 1 of 12, australia 1 of 17 and germany 1 of 14.

the US loans that got stuck this autumn at least have a bond market to go to once the building is done. a lot of these don’t have an obvious one, so the loan probably stays with whichever bank wrote it for a lot longer.

it’s hard to know how much construction risk is sitting there, because that’s exactly the part nobody outside the deal can see. but the further this build gets from the US, the less visible it gets, and the more of it probably ends up on a bank balance sheet with no obvious exit.引用原帖（@nodiligence）：credit markets still look calm about AI, probably because most of the build isn’t in them yet. it’s in short bank loans waiting for a bond to pay them off, and power and permits keep pushing that back. three of the biggest are already stuck with banks. https://t.co/A722Vr0dOa 链接

2026-11-11 19:41 北京时间 ｜ Source: @nodiligence ｜ 原帖链接

$MS $GS $JPM $DB $SMFG $MUFG $BNP $ACA $ORCL

我追踪了地图上每家大银行所在的每一笔 AI 数据中心融资，并统计有多少笔能看到任何人都可以核实的现金。这些银行按商业模式分得相当清楚。

德意志银行、摩根士丹利、摩根大通和高盛，有 41% 到 53% 的交易显示出现金。他们经手的很多是自己安排并直接卖给投资者的债券，所以先拿费用，再把风险转出去。

SMBC 和 MUFG 分别有 27% 和 21% 的交易显示出现金，贷款占其交易的 62% 和 85%。Natixis、BNP Paribas 和 Crédit Agricole 只有 0% 到 13%，贷款占比为 76% 到 100%。这些银行是放贷并持有，一旦停滞，卖不掉的部分——也就是建设那一块——就留在自己手里。

合计来看，日资或法资银行手上有 950 亿美元看不到已提取金额的美元贷款。MUFG 牵头了 Oracle 380 亿美元的 Vantage 方案，是 180 亿美元 Jupiter 贷款的代理行，并向得州与 Anthropic 相关的 Nexus 过桥投入了约 20 亿美元。这样一来，新墨西哥的电力延误和得州的许可冻结，就落在同一张资产负债表上。而 SMBC 自己的 James Martin 在 10 月对路透表示，投资者更青睐更接近完工的数据中心交易，这就会把离完工最远的那些留在持有人手里。

这些银行想退出时，可以做风险转移，付钱让别人承担第一损失。Octus 在 7 月报道称，买方往往是已经持有同一栋楼股权的基金，价格在 7% 到 9%，比其他基础设施的 8% 到 12% 更紧。风险最终落到已经拥有该资产的人身上，而定价假设大楼会被建成，并且离任何监管者更远。

监管已经开始关注。日本金融厅（FSA）已在计划检查美国数据中心项目融资，欧洲央行至少从 2 月起就在询问欧洲贷款人的敞口。不过两家都不会按银行公布已提取金额，所以从外部很难知道任何一家银行被卡住的那一块到底有多大。

如果想通过银行获得 AI 融资敞口，我更偏好把票据卖出去的那些，而不是把票据留在自己账上的那些。

原文：$MS $GS $JPM $DB $SMFG $MUFG $BNP $ACA $ORCL

i tracked every AI data-centre financing each big bank sits on in my map and counted how many show cash anyone can check. the banks split pretty cleanly by business model.

deutsche bank, morgan stanley, jpmorgan and goldman show cash on 41% to 53% of their deals. a lot of what they touch is bonds they arranged and sold straight on to investors, so they take the fee up front and pass the risk on.

SMBC and MUFG show cash on 27% and 21%, with loans at 62% and 85% of their deals. natixis, BNP paribas and crédit agricole are at 0% to 13%, with loans at 76% to 100%. these banks lend and hold, and in a stall they keep whatever doesn’t sell, which is the construction piece.

between them, japanese or french banks sit on $95bn of the US-dollar loans with no drawn figure. MUFG led oracle’s $38bn vantage package, is an agent on the $18bn jupiter loan, and put about $2bn into the anthropic-linked nexus bridge in texas. that puts a power delay in new mexico and a permit freeze in texas on the same balance sheet. and SMBC’s own james martin told reuters in october that investors favour data-centre deals closer to completion, which leaves the holders with whatever is furthest from it.

when these banks want out, they can do a risk transfer and pay someone to take the first losses. octus reported in july that the buyers are often funds that already own equity in the same buildings, at 7% to 9%, tighter than the 8% to 12% on other infrastructure. the risk ends up with someone who already owns the asset, at a price that assumes the building gets finished, and further from any regulator.

the regulators are starting to look. japan’s FSA is already planning inspections of US data-centre project finance, and the ECB has been asking european lenders about their exposure since at least february. neither publishes drawn amounts by bank, though, so from outside it’s hard to know how big any one bank’s stuck piece really is.

if you want AI financing exposure through banks, i prefer the ones that sell the paper over the ones that keep it.引用原帖（@nodiligence）：credit markets still look calm about AI, probably because most of the build isn’t in them yet. it’s in short bank loans waiting for a bond to pay them off, and power and permits keep pushing that back. three of the biggest are already stuck with banks. https://t.co/A722Vr0dOa 链接

这应当引起大量关注，并给 neocloud 带来额外压力，因为算力价格是有上限的。

2026-11-11 21:19 北京时间 ｜ Source: @RihardJarc ｜ 原帖链接

这表明 $ANT——这个因为利润率最好而付得起最高算力价格的买方——不愿意按现在这些价格买算力。这意味着你不能把这些算力价格水平建模给 neocloud；再加上债务利息，这些商业模式的盈利能力就变得可疑。市场不想看到 neocloud 去做那些业务指标和利润说不通的大额交易。在 neocloud 这件事上，市场已经走过了只看“收入和 backlog”的阶段，现在紧盯经济学本身，而这件事对此没有帮助。

原文：It shows that $ANT, the buyer who can afford to pay the highest price compute-wise because it has the best margin, is not willing to pay compute prices at these levels. This means that you can't model these compute price levels for the neoclouds, and with debt interest, the business models become questionable in terms of profitability. The market doesn't want neoclouds doing big-volume deals with business metrics and profits that don't make sense. The market, when it comes to neoclouds, has moved past the "revenue and backlog" days and is laser-focused on economics and this doesn't help that.所回复原帖（@JaeKim1089208）：@RihardJarc Even if what Jukan said is true, how does this put additional pressure on neoclouds? @GavinSBaker（保留 1 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 1 条）

2026-11-11 07:33 北京时间 ｜ Source: @GavinSBaker ｜ 原帖链接

融资方式从用 offtake 协议为 GPU 集群融资，转向 prepayment，这意味着 OCF to EBITDA 成了最重要的指标。

尽职调查的负担，也从评估 offtaker 信用资质的私募信贷基金，转移到实际使用 GPU、并支付 prepayment 的客户身上。

对于越来越多近乎 100% 预付的客户来说，dock to live（从到货到上线）以及历史集群稳定性水平，将是最重要的指标。

这也显著改善了 GPU 的回收期。

原文：The shift away from financing GPU clusters with offtake agreements to prepayments means that OCF to EBITDA is the most important metric.

Moves the diligence burden from private credit funds evaluating the creditworthiness of offtaker to the actual customer of the GPU making the prepayment.

Dock to live and historic cluster stability levels will be the most important metrics to customers who are increasingly paying nearly 100% upfront.

Also significantly improves the payback period on GPUs.

2026-11-12 05:50 北京时间 ｜ Source: @SouthernValue95 ｜ 原帖链接

很有意思——$GOOG 的 TPU 拥有 AI 计算的最佳架构，而且“TPU 不够用”。

$AVGO $ANT

“今天我会说，大概最好的架构是 Google 的 TPU 架构，因为它从一开始就是按单一系统来构思的。我觉得 NVIDIA 风格的架构正在朝 TPU 架构的方向迁移，但仍然有一些可以替换的部件。然后就是其他所有人——比如，我拿一颗 Cerebras 芯片，再在上面加一个 Arista 交换机，诸如此类。至少在今天，这些系统有效率损失，因为它们不是作为系统被构建的，而更像是拼凑起来的。

我认为行业会演进，但今天，能够把整件事看成单一系统并据此优化架构的人，拥有竞争优势。

看看 Google 怎么做：用这些环形直通的光学 MEMS 交换机——简直就是整个数据中心才是最小单元。但我的感觉是，NVIDIA 也想走到那里。实际上，两个主导供应商都在走向这种系统方法，甚至较小的初创公司也在往这个方向走。

现在 Jensen 是山上之王，极其主导，对吧？Google TPU 非常成功，目前主要由 Google 自己销售——预计这一点会改变。但 TPU 不够用。”

原文：Fascinating - $GOOG TPU has the best architecture for AI compute, and “there aren’t enough TPUs,”

$AVGO $ANT

“Today I would argue that probably the best architecture is the Google TPU architecture, because it is literally thought of as a single system. And I think an NVIDIA-style architecture is migrating in the direction of where the TPU architecture is, but it still has a few pieces that you can kind of swap out. And then you have everybody else — like, you know, I'm going to take a Cerebras chip and throw an Arista switch on top of it, and all that good stuff. At least in today's world, those systems have inefficiencies because they're not constructed as a system — they're more of a patchwork.

I think the industry will evolve, but today, people that can think of the whole thing as a single system and optimize the architecture through that have a competitive advantage.

If you look at how Google does this, with these toroidal pass-through optical MEMS switches — it's literally like the whole data center is the minimum unit. But my sense is that's where NVIDIA wants to go also. Actually, the two dominant providers are all going to that systems approach, versus — I mean, even the smaller startups are heading that direction.

Right now Jensen's king of the hill, incredibly dominant, right? Google TPU very successful, currently mostly sold by Google — it's expected to change. But not enough TPUs.”引用原帖（@?）：Super fund to chat with @RaghuRaghuram &amp;

Altman 说：“真正在大幅拉动需求增长的，只有 OpenAI 和 Anthropic 两家，所以我们可能会看到一次供给冲击，以及一段动荡期。”

原文：During a Q&A with Samsung executives, Sam Altman addressed concerns about a bubble in the neocloud sector, saying, “There has been some double counting of demand.”

Asked whether demand for AI, memory chips, and data centers could level off or grow more slowly over the next five to ten years, he replied, “There are ups and downs in the short term.”

“OpenAI and Anthropic are the only two companies really driving substantial growth in demand, so we could see a supply shock and a period of turbulence,” Altman said.

2026-11-11 21:02 北京时间 ｜ Source: @jukan05 ｜ 原帖链接

Sam Altman 说：“长期来看，对 AI 的需求会极大增长。” 他预期，随着 AI 实时编写定制软件，并更广泛地用于机器人、航天器和科学研究，AI 模型处理的信息单位——tokens 的用量将急剧上升。

Altman 说：“未来几年 AI 扩张的最大约束，将是芯片供应链和数据中心基础设施。我们需要加大对供应链的投资。”

三星高管问，OpenAI 持续的大规模投资是否在给盈利能力带来压力，Altman 回答：“这就是关键。如果我们能以比任何人更低的成本建成垂直整合的基础设施，并以薄利高量出售 tokens，我们就能跑在竞争前面。”

Altman 说：“这个市场太大了，商品业务的通常规则并不适用。只要能满足全球对 tokens 的需求，就足以撑起一个繁荣的行业。”

原文：“Demand for AI will grow enormously over the long term,” Sam Altman said. He expects the use of tokens—the units of information AI models process—to rise sharply as AI writes custom software in real time and finds wider use in robotics, spacecraft, and scientific research.

“The biggest constraints on AI expansion over the next few years will be the chip supply chain and data center infrastructure,” Altman said. “We need more investment in the supply chain.”

Asked by Samsung executives whether OpenAI’s continued heavy investment was putting pressure on profitability, Altman replied, “That’s the crux of it. If we can build vertically integrated infrastructure at a lower cost than anyone else and sell tokens in high volumes at thin margins, we can get ahead of the competition.”

“The market is so large that the usual rules of a commodity business don’t apply,” Altman said. “Just meeting global demand for tokens would be enough to build a thriving industry.”所回复原帖（@jukan05）：During a Q&A with Samsung executives, Sam Altman addressed concerns about a bubble in the neocloud sector, saying, “There has been some double counting of demand.”

2026-11-12 02:03 北京时间 ｜ Source: @SemiAnalysis_ ｜ 原帖链接

Google 发布了 TPU 8i Max，又称 Whalefish。一个有意思的细节是：此次宣布确认 8i Max 每芯片将有 384GB HBM，意味着它改用 8-hi HBM，而原来的 TPU 8i 用的是 12-hi HBM——这一点我们几个月前就已向 Accelerator Model 订阅者报告过。这追随了 Nvidia 将下一代 Rubin Ultra 改用 8-hi HBM 的做法。

Google 宣传 TPU 8i Max 的推理性能要强得多，因为每个封装现在拥有两倍的 HBM 带宽。带宽对推理性能的重要性高于容量，我们最近在 4-hi HBM 的文章里解释过，可在此阅读：https://t.co/HBWMhtJzy6

原文：Google has unveiled TPU 8i Max, aka Whalefish. One interesting detail is that the announcement confirms the 8i Max will have 384GB of HBM per chip, meaning it moves to 8-hi HBM compared to the 12-hi HBM used for the original TPU 8i, which we reported to Accelerator Model subscribers several months ago. This follows Nvidia's move to move to 8-hi HBM for next generation Rubin Ultra.

Google is advertising much stronger inference performance TPU 8i Max as each package now has double the HBM bandwidth. Bandwidth is more important than capacity for inference performance as we explained recently in our 4-hi HBM article which you can read here: https://t.co/HBWMhtJzy6

$CRWV 财报及对 $NBIS 和其他 Neocloud 的 read through：

“我们合格需求是可用产能的 10 倍。”

- 对 2028 年产能的兴趣，已经相当于 CoreWeave 可用量的数倍

- CoreWeave 在 7 月提价 25%，三季度晚些时候再加 10%，四季度还会再提

- Ampere 和 Hopper 租赁价格环比上涨 30–50%（较老的 GPU）

- 融资条件改善（更高的 prepayment，新贷款利率降至 7–7.5%）

- 2028 年上半年实现正的调整后净利润

所以我们看到定价在走高，尤其是较老的 GPU……融资机制改善，以及极端的需求失衡：需求是供给的 10 倍。

这家公司本身在利率上仍有同样的问题，但我认为其他 Neocloud 处境更好。

不过，眼看着 AI 基础设施公司股价暴跌、而需求还在增长，还是很疯狂。

原文：$CRWV ER and read through for $NBIS and other Neoclouds:

“We have 10x as much qualified demand as available capacity”.

- interest in 2028 capacity already amounts to several times what CoreWeave has available

- CoreWeave raised prices by 25% in July, +10% later in Q3, and more to come in Q4

- Ampere and Hopper rental pricing rose 30–50% quarter over quarter (older GPUs)

- better financing conditions (higher prepayments, lower interest 7–7.5% for new loans)

- positive adjusted net income in the first half of 2028.

So we’re seeing higher pricing esp. for older GPUs…better financing mechanics, and extreme demand imbalance where you have 10x demand > supply.

Same issues are there for this company in specific with interest rates, but I’d argue other Neoclouds are in a better spot.

But it’s wild to witness AI infrastructure names crash while demand keeps growing

- $SMTC generated 74% of its revenue through distributors. $AMBA as another example, ~60-70% of their 2026 revenue went through this company.

- With WT in specific, if you look at even a large company like $AVGO scaled:

-> 13% of Broadcom revenue went through WT in 2020
-> 18% (2021)
-> 20% (2022)
-> ~21% (2023)
-> ~28% (2024)
-> now possibly up to ~46% (they stopped naming)

Just showing how important the largest distributors in the world become to semi companies.

- $GOOGL Cloud 将基于 Arm 的 $GOOGL Axion 处理器扩展为 TPU 8i Max 的 host CPU，此前已部署于 TPU 8t 和 TPU 8i。

- Axion N4A 相较同类 x86 VM，性价比提升 2 倍；运行在 Axion 上的 GKE Agent Sandbox 在安全隔离工作负载的同时，性价比提升 30%。

- GKE 上的 Agent Substrate 现已原生支持 Axion，与标准容器运行时相比，sandbox 密度提高 10 倍，用于规模化部署 agentic AI 工作负载。

> $GOOGL 以 Axion 与 TPU 8i Max 做 CPU-加速器协同设计，凸显出对 agentic AI 工作负载而言，host CPU 性能的重要性日益上升，已不只是纯加速器吞吐量。

> 这也强化了 Arm 在 AI 基础设施中的战略定位：host 计算效率会直接影响规模化条件下的数据准备、工作负载编排和 agentic reasoning 任务。

原文：- $GOOGL Cloud extends Arm-based $GOOGL Axion processors as host CPU for TPU 8i Max, following prior deployments in TPU 8t and TPU 8i.

- Axion N4A delivers 2x better price-performance versus comparable x86 VMs; GKE Agent Sandbox on Axion achieves 30% better price-performance with secure workload isolation.

- Agent Substrate on GKE now natively supports Axion, enabling 10x higher sandbox density compared to standard container runtimes for scaled agentic AI workload deployment.

> $GOOGL's CPU-accelerator co-design approach with Axion and TPU 8i Max underscores the growing importance of host CPU performance for agentic AI workloads beyond pure accelerator throughput.

> This reinforces Arm's strategic positioning in AI infrastructure, where host compute efficiency directly impacts data preparation, workload orchestration, and agentic reasoning tasks at scale.

- $GOOGL 已将 TPU 客户群扩大到近 100 家。

> 关键区分在于：是削弱 $AVGO 的采购角色，还是替换其 TPU 设计合作伙伴关系。

> 投资者不仅应跟踪 TPU 出货量，还应关注每颗 TPU 的 HBM 容量，以及供应商市场份额的变化。

- $ORCL plans to offer Google TPUs through OCI starting in 2027.

- $AVGO maintains a long-term agreement to develop and supply Google's next-generation TPUs through 2031.

> The key distinction is between reducing $AVGO's procurement role and replacing its TPU design partnership.

> Investors should monitor not only TPU shipments but also HBM capacity per TPU and changes in supplier market share.

- 管理层认为 OCS 有可能成为年收入数十亿美元的业务。

> OCS 可能正从早期部署，转向一个大型商业市场。

- 其芯片级 OCS 目标是切换时间低于 5 微秒。

- Voyant 计划供应 OCS 引擎，而不是完整的网络系统。

- 关键开发优先级包括非阻塞拓扑、光损耗、串扰和封装。

> LiDAR 光子交换技术正在扩展进入 AI 数据中心基础设施。

> 仅有更快的器件切换，并不能保证更好的网络吞吐。调度和重配置开销仍然关键。

- OCP 于 2026 年发布了 Fast-Switching OCS 白皮书。

- 贡献者包括 $AMD、$INTC、$CIEN、$LITE、iPronics 和 Lumotive。

- 一份 2026 年 4 月的 OCP 论文比较了 MEMS 与 SiPh 交换技术。

> OCS 竞争正从相对静态的光路，扩展到动态的 AI fabric。

> 端到端重配置时间和网络软件成熟度，比孤立的器件切换速度更重要。

- $COHR 提供基于数字液晶的 OCS。

- 其配置从 64x64 到 512x512 端口。

- 公司已演示过 300x300 端口系统。

> OCS 竞争已不限于 MEMS 对 SiPh，液晶正在成为另一种高 radix 选项。

> 端口成本、插入损耗、重配置时间和客户采用情况，将塑造 $LITE 与 $COHR 之间的竞争。

> 核心变化是：OCS 正在从 Google 以 MEMS 为中心的架构向外多样化。

> MEMS 在高端口数和低损耗上有吸引力，而 SiPh 则瞄准更快的重配置和半导体制造的可扩展性。

> 投资者应跟踪 $LITE 实际的 OCS 营收增长、$COHR 的客户采用，以及 $TSEM 向放量制造的过渡。

- Its chip-scale OCS targets switching times below 5 microseconds.

- Voyant plans to supply OCS engines rather than complete networking systems.

- Key development priorities include non-blocking topology, optical loss, crosstalk, and packaging.

> LiDAR photonic switching technology is expanding into AI data center infrastructure.

> Fast device switching alone does not guarantee better network throughput. Scheduling and reconfiguration overhead remain critical.

- Contributors include $AMD, $INTC, $CIEN, $LITE, iPronics, and Lumotive.

- An April 2026 OCP paper compared MEMS and SiPh switching technologies.

> OCS competition is expanding from relatively static optical paths toward dynamic AI fabrics.

> End-to-end reconfiguration time and network software maturity matter more than isolated device switching speed.

- $COHR offers digital liquid crystal-based OCS.

- Its configurations range from 64x64 to 512x512 ports.

- The company has demonstrated a 300x300-port system.

> OCS competition extends beyond MEMS versus SiPh, with liquid crystal emerging as another high-radix option.

> Port cost, insertion loss, reconfiguration time, and customer adoption will shape competition between $LITE and $COHR.

> The central shift is OCS diversification beyond Google's MEMS-centered architecture.

> MEMS offers attractive high-port-count and low-loss characteristics, while SiPh targets faster reconfiguration and semiconductor manufacturing scalability.

> Investors should monitor $LITE's actual OCS revenue growth, $COHR's customer adoption, and $TSEM's transition into volume manufacturing.

- $GOOGL 在 2010 年代开始开发 OCS，并将其部署在 Jupiter 数据中心网络和 TPU 基础设施中。

- Google 称，其由 OCS 和 SDN 赋能的 Jupiter 架构，使网络 CAPEX 降低 30%，功耗降低 41%。

- OCS 在交换层重新配置光连接，而无需把光信号转换成电信号。

- $MSFT 于 2025 年加入 OCP 的 OCS 倡议，与 Google、$NVDA、$LITE 和 $COHR 并列。

- $META 开发了 Disaggregated Scheduled Fabric，以应对传统 Clos 网络的扩展局限，并在探索替代交换技术。

> Google 长达十年的 OCS 部署，为 AI 数据中心更广泛的采用奠定了已获验证的基础。

> 不断增大的 AI 集群，正在暴露电分组交换在功耗、容量和成本上的约束。

- $AMZN 于 2026 年 5 月推出基于 ShuffleBox 的扁平网络架构，但这种无源光互连方法与 OCS 并不相同。

- $META 在 2026 年 10 月披露的 Prometheus 基础设施中，仍大量依赖以太网分组交换。

> 公开披露尚未表明 $MSFT、$AMZN 和 $META 将大规模转向全光交换。

> 不过，电分组交换成本上升，增强了用 OCS 替换部分交换层的经济理由。

> 如果采用从 Google 扩展到多家 hyperscaler，OCS 市场机会可能急剧扩大。

> $LITE 和 $COHR 提供直接的 OCS 硬件敞口，而基于 SiPh 的架构可能为 $TSEM 创造额外的制造机会。

> 比参与 OCS 倡议的公司数量更重要的，是实际投产部署、端口出货以及向收入的转化。

- Google reported 30% lower networking CAPEX and 41% lower power consumption from its OCS- and SDN-enabled Jupiter architecture.

- OCS reconfigures optical connections without converting optical signals into electrical signals at the switching layer.

- $MSFT joined the OCP OCS initiative alongside Google, $NVDA, $LITE, and $COHR in 2025.

- $META developed Disaggregated Scheduled Fabric to address the scaling limitations of traditional Clos networks and is exploring alternative switching technologies.

> Google's decade-long OCS deployment establishes a proven foundation for broader adoption across AI data centers.

> Growing AI clusters are exposing the power, capacity, and cost constraints of electrical packet switching.

- $AMZN introduced its ShuffleBox-based flat network architecture in May 2026, but this passive optical interconnect approach is distinct from OCS.

- $META continues to rely heavily on Ethernet packet switching in its Prometheus infrastructure, disclosed in October 2026.

> A wholesale transition by $MSFT, $AMZN, and $META to all-optical switching is not yet established by public disclosures.

> However, rising electrical packet switching costs strengthen the economic case for replacing selected switching layers with OCS.

> The OCS market opportunity could expand dramatically if adoption extends beyond Google to multiple hyperscalers.

> $LITE and $COHR offer direct OCS hardware exposure, while SiPh-based architectures could create additional manufacturing opportunities for $TSEM.

- EV Group（EVG）宣布，通过先进封装和异构集成，扩大对量子计算的支持。

- 关键技术包括 fusion bonding、hybrid bonding、高真空无氧化物键合、纳米压印光刻，以及薄晶圆处理。

- 对于光子量子计算，EVG 支持将 III-V 族以及高 Pockels 系数材料与光波导进行异构集成。

- Hybrid bonding 可实现高密度自旋量子比特互连，而低温金属键合则支持离子阱 chiplet 集成。

- EVG 于 2026 年 5 月与 Xanadu（$XNDU）合作，开发工业级光子量子硬件制造。

- EVG 还参与 CHAMP-ION，这是一条面向工业级离子阱量子芯片生产的欧洲中试线。

> 量子扩展的瓶颈正在从量子比特数量，扩展到在不牺牲性能的前提下集成异构材料和芯片的制造技术。

> 对于光子量子计算，关键挑战是在保持晶圆级良率的同时，将光源和电光材料与低损耗波导集成在一起。

> EVG 正在把自身定位于 AI CPO 与量子光子学所共享的晶圆键合和异构集成供应链之中。

> 它与 Xanadu 的合作意味着从实验室制备向工业级光子量子制造过渡，不过量产良率和商业规模仍未得到确认。

原文：- EV Group (EVG) announced expanded support for quantum computing through advanced packaging and heterogeneous integration.

- Key technologies include fusion bonding, hybrid bonding, high-vacuum oxide-free bonding, nanoimprint lithography, and thin-wafer processing.

- For photonic quantum computing, EVG supports heterogeneous integration of III-V and high-Pockels-coefficient materials with optical waveguides.

- Hybrid bonding enables dense spin-qubit interconnects, while low-temperature metal bonding supports trapped-ion chiplet integration.

- EVG partnered with Xanadu ($XNDU) in May 2026 to develop industrial-scale photonic quantum hardware manufacturing.

- EVG also participates in CHAMP-ION, a European pilot line for industrial-scale trapped-ion quantum chip production.

> Quantum scaling bottlenecks are expanding beyond qubit counts to manufacturing technologies that integrate heterogeneous materials and chips without compromising performance.

> For photonic quantum computing, the critical challenge is integrating light sources and electro-optic materials with low-loss waveguides while maintaining wafer-level yield.

> EVG is positioning itself within the wafer bonding and heterogeneous integration supply chain shared by AI CPO and quantum photonics.

> Its Xanadu partnership suggests a transition from laboratory fabrication toward industrial-scale photonic quantum manufacturing, although production yields and commercial volumes remain unconfirmed.

- SK hynix 的 LPDDR5X DRAM 获得 IEC 61508 SIL 3 功能安全认证，这是单一存储半导体可获得的最高级别。
- 公司还获得了 ISO/SAE 21434 网络安全认证，覆盖从开发到退役的产品全生命周期威胁管理。
- 这些认证将安全资质从汽车扩展到机器人与智能工厂；continuous-mode 设计可支持实时安全关键应用。

> SK hynix 全面的功能安全与网络安全认证，回应了物理 AI 和自动驾驶系统的关键要求——在这些场景中，存储器数据完整性直接影响运行安全。
> 这些多标准认证，为其向需要严格安全验证的机器人和出行平台供应边缘存储器，提供了竞争差异化。

原文：- SK hynix's LPDDR5X DRAM obtained IEC 61508 SIL 3 functional safety certification, the highest level for a single memory semiconductor.
- Company also secured ISO/SAE 21434 cybersecurity certification, covering threat management across product lifecycle from development to decommissioning.
- Certifications extend safety credentials beyond automotive to robotics and smart factories; continuous-mode design enables real-time safety-critical applications.

> SK hynix's comprehensive functional safety and cybersecurity certifications address critical requirements for physical AI and autonomous systems where memory data integrity directly impacts operational safety.
> These multi-standard certifications provide competitive differentiation in supplying edge memory for robotics and mobility platforms requiring rigorous safety validation.

2026-11-11 08:27 北京时间 ｜ Source: @benitoz ｜ 原帖链接

Marcelo 我喜欢你，但为什么人们因为 Anthropic 在三天内让模型的一部分在 AMD 上跑起来，就不断说“CUDA is dead”？

这确实了不起。但据此宣布 NVIDIA 的护城河已经消失，就是胡扯。

CUDA 不是你移植过去的几行代码。它是一个庞大的库、工具生态系统，以及多年的优化，开发者将其用于 AI、科学、机器人等领域。

正如 John Linford 和我在视频中讨论的（链接见下），让某个东西在另一块芯片上跑起来是一回事。让整个工作负载在规模上可靠运行，并具备有竞争力的性能和成本，则是另一回事。

竞争是真实存在的，AI 也会让使用其他硬件变得更容易。但这并不意味着 NVIDIA 数十年来打造的整个生态系统就这样消失了。

原文：Love you Marcelo but why do people keep saying “CUDA is dead” because Anthropic got part of a model running on AMD in three days.

That’s impressive. But declaring NVIDIA’s moat gone from that is bullshit.

CUDA isn’t just a few lines of code you port over. It’s a massive ecosystem of libraries, tools and years of optimization that developers use across AI, science, robotics and more.

As John Linford and I discussed in our video (link below) getting something to run on another chip is one thing. Getting the whole workload running reliably at scale, with competitive performance and cost, is another.

Competition is real, and AI will make it easier to use other hardware. That doesn’t mean the entire ecosystem NVIDIA built over decades just disappeared.

https://t.co/HECEpGmICg引用原帖（@MarceloLima）：The Nvidia CUDA moat is gone. Anthropic sees all compute as fungible and it only took Mythos 3 days to figure out how to run itself on AMD hardware. https://t.co/ki8lv0t8Fu 链接

2026-11-11 11:16 北京时间 ｜ Source: @benitoz ｜ 原帖链接

Arash，“这些优化大多已经不再必要”这句话在这里承担了太多分量。

模型仍然需要搬运数据、在加速器之间通信，并在延迟和成本目标内提供 tokens。你可以把这种复杂性藏在一个 agent 或框架后面，但系统仍然得把这些工作做完。

如果这些优化已经不再重要，那就给我看对等条件下、规模化的吞吐量、延迟、功耗和每 token 成本。我很乐意看这些数据。

原文：Arash, “Most of these optimizations are no longer necessary” is doing a lot of work here.

The model still has to move data, communicate across accelerators and serve tokens within a latency and cost target. You can hide that complexity behind an agent or a framework, but the system still has to do the work.

If the optimizations don’t matter anymore, show me the apples-to-apples throughput, latency, power and cost per token at scale. I’m happy to look at the data.所回复原帖（@donarashh）：most of these optimizations are no longer necessary/relevant wrt model execution https://t.co/viAT8CU0OB 链接

🔔 The Daily Semi-Cap — 2026 年 11 月 10 日

📊 SMH：$483.63（较前一交易日收盘 -0.78%）

• CoreWeave 的 Q3 营收跳升 168%，至 36.6 亿美元；backlog 达到 1325 亿美元，2026 年营收指引上调至 130–133 亿美元。在运电力超过 1.7GW。需求不是问题：当季净利息费用 8.46 亿美元，远超经营利润 2.07 亿美元，融资经济性仍是焦点。

• 软银在修订美国 AI 数据中心电力设备采购计划（包括其俄亥俄园区）后，确认了 37.3 亿美元亏损。其中包括 28.5 亿美元不可退还的预付款，以及 8.80 亿美元的合同修改与和解。为稀缺发电产能做预定，在项目需求发生变化时会带来真实的下行风险——并非每一笔设备承诺最终都会变成已部署的兆瓦。

• 台积电 10 月营收达到 NT$5868.9 亿，同比增 59.7%，环比增 14.7%。尽管 AI 股票遭到抛售，这家代工厂进入第四季度时仍给出又一个强劲的出货信号。这些是已经实现的销售，而不是客户的支出意向，进一步凸显了半导体经营动能与日益紧张的基础设施投资者之间的落差。

• AMD 已将第六代 EPYC SP7 处理器全面上市，OEM/ODM 系统已在出货，Azure 和 Oracle 云实例即将推出。Zen 6 刷新强化了其 CPU 地位，因 agents 需要协调 GPU 工作负载并运行应用和数据库。AI 基础设施支出并不只是一场加速器竞赛。

• 英飞凌财年第四季度营收 47.1 亿欧元，segment-result 利润率 23.8%。2027 财年指引目标约 200 亿欧元营收，数据中心电源销售接近 40 亿欧元，投资 30–35 亿欧元。功率半导体正在成为与汽车和工业复苏并行的、大得多的增长引擎。

• 微软正在向华盛顿施压，反对限制中国光模块，指出这是 GPU 和电力之外又一个潜在的部署瓶颈。官员们在考虑以 3.2Tbps 为门槛，而不是一刀切切断。Coherent 和 Lumentum 可能从本土化中受益，但在替代供应尚未放量之前就替换中国供应，有可能拖慢美国的安装进度。

• 佳能宣布将于 12 月初推出 FPA-6400ES6a KrF 扫描仪，规格为每小时 400 片晶圆、4nm 套刻精度。该设备还可容纳高达 700 微米的晶圆翘曲。更高通量的光刻对存储和逻辑产能很重要；这是成熟曝光技术上的竞争，而不是 EUV 的替代品。

原文：🔔 The Daily Semi-Cap — November 10, 2026

📊 SMH: $483.63 (-0.78% vs prior trading close)

• CoreWeave’s Q3 revenue jumped 168% to $3.66B; backlog reached $132.5B and 2026 revenue guidance rose to $13.0–13.3B. Active power exceeds 1.7GW. Demand is not the problem: $846M of quarterly net interest expense dwarfed $207M of operating income, keeping financing economics firmly in focus.

• SoftBank recognized a $3.73B loss after revising procurement plans for US AI-datacenter power equipment, including its Ohio campus. That comprises $2.85B of non-refundable advances and $880M of contract modifications and settlements. Reserving scarce generation capacity carries real downside when project requirements change—not every equipment commitment becomes deployed megawatts.

• TSMC’s October revenue hit NT$586.89B, up 59.7% year over year and 14.7% sequentially. The foundry enters Q4 with another strong shipment signal despite the AI-stock selloff. These are realized sales, not customer spending intentions, reinforcing the gap between semiconductor operating momentum and increasingly nervous infrastructure investors.

• AMD made sixth-generation EPYC SP7 processors generally available, with OEM/ODM systems shipping and Azure and Oracle cloud instances coming. The Zen 6 refresh strengthens its CPU position as agents coordinate GPU workloads and run applications and databases. AI infrastructure spending is not exclusively an accelerator contest.

• Infineon delivered €4.71B of fiscal Q4 revenue at a 23.8% segment-result margin. FY2027 guidance targets approximately €20B revenue, with datacenter power-supply sales approaching €4B and €3.0–3.5B of investment. Power semiconductors are becoming a much larger growth engine alongside automotive and industrial recovery.

• Microsoft is pressing Washington against restricting Chinese optical transceivers, highlighting another potential deployment bottleneck beyond GPUs and electricity. Officials are considering a 3.2Tbps threshold rather than a sweeping cutoff. Coherent and Lumentum could benefit from localization, but replacing Chinese supply before alternatives scale risks slowing US installations.

• Canon announced its FPA-6400ES6a KrF scanner for early-December launch, specifying 400 wafers per hour and 4nm overlay accuracy. The tool also accommodates wafer warpage up to 700 microns. Higher-throughput lithography matters for memory and logic capacity; this is competition in mature exposure technology, not an EUV replacement.

原文：KLA and Onto both say DRAM pricing is hurting their gross margins. But both say they are redesigning their inspection and metrology tools to use less memory. Inspection and metrology companies in general have higher GM vs etch, deposition and litho peers.

token 可以更便宜，但一项任务仍然很贵吗？

一个 agent 既可能把 token 花在解决问题上，也可能反复花在修补自己的错误上。模型质量、重试、工具和人工复核都很重要。

每美元能买到的 token 数，讲不完整个 ROI 故事。

5/

原文：Can tokens get cheaper while a task stays expensive?

An agent can spend tokens solving a problem, or repeatedly repairing its own mistakes. Model quality, retries, tools and human review all matter.

Tokens per dollar cannot tell the whole ROI story.

5/ https://x.com/IAmAdiFuchs/status/2120551197060854054/photo/1所回复原帖（@IAmAdiFuchs）：An AI chip can lose money without doing a single calculation.

At an assumed $37K over five years: $0.84 per available hour at 100% uptime; but $3.38 at 25% uptime. CapEx only.

The worst thing we can do is let it sit idle. Availability still has to become useful work.

@damnang2（保留 1 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 26 条）

2026-11-11 11:49 北京时间 ｜ Source: @damnang2 ｜ 原帖链接

在这次专家通话中，我与一位在存储制造商和半导体设备供应商拥有数十年经验的行业资深人士进行了交流。他们的工作聚焦于存储技术和路线图，包括存储厂商正在投资哪些方向，以及这些技术进入量产之前需要解决什么问题。

我们讨论了 4F² 和 3D DRAM 如何延长 DRAM 微缩，以及客户需求如何塑造 HBM 和 3D 堆叠 DRAM 的开发。我们还探讨了为什么各家存储厂商在优先事项、以及计划何时采用新技术上存在差异，以及这些决策如何塑造设备路线图。

整个对话贯穿着一个问题：下一代 DRAM 何时会在经济上说得通？一项技术可以在实验室里很早就跑通，但要等到良率、制造成本和客户需求能够支撑量产，往往还需要很长时间。希望这次讨论能给你一种方法，去阅读未来的路线图公告，判断哪些技术正在靠近量产，以及还有哪些条件需要到位。

原文：For this expert call, I spoke with an industry veteran with decades of experience at memory manufacturers and semiconductor equipment suppliers. Their work focuses on memory technology and roadmaps, including where memory makers are investing and what they need to solve before those technologies can move into volume production.

We discussed how 4F² and 3D DRAM could extend DRAM scaling, and how customer requirements are shaping the development of HBM and 3D stacked DRAM. We also looked at why memory makers differ in what they prioritize and when they plan to adopt new technologies, and how those decisions shape equipment roadmaps.

One question ran through the conversation. When will next generation DRAM make economic sense? A technology can work in the lab long before yields, manufacturing costs, and customer demand support volume production. I hope this discussion gives you a way to read future roadmap announcements and judge which technologies are getting closer to volume production and what still needs to fall into place.引用原帖（@damnang2）：https://t.co/c7jMgUCVwY 链接

为什么 NPO 不仅仅是通往 CPO 的中间步骤
人们很容易把光网络描述成一条直线演进：

可插拔光模块 → NPO → CPO

实际上，这一转型涉及相互竞争的系统优先级。

原文：Why NPO Is More Than an Intermediate Step Toward CPO
It is tempting to describe optical networking as a straightforward progression:

Pluggable Optics → NPO → CPO

In practice, the transition involves competing system priorities.

2026-11-11 14:53 北京时间 ｜ Source: @semivision_tw ｜ 原帖链接

因此，先进失效分析的关键价值，并不只是把异常检测出来。

而是缩短从失效检测到定位根因并采取纠正措施所需的时间。

对先进封装制造商而言，这种能力可以直接影响产品开发进度、良率爬升和制造可靠性。

原文：The critical value of advanced failure analysis is therefore not simply detecting abnormalities.

It is reducing the time required to move from failure detection to root-cause identification and corrective action.

For advanced packaging manufacturers, this capability can directly affect product development schedules, yield ramp-up, and manufacturing reliability.

★@sundarpichai（保留 4 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 5 条）

2026-11-12 02:15 北京时间 ｜ Source: ★@sundarpichai ｜ 原帖链接

我们今天发布 Gemini Decisions API，由我们的新模型 Gemini Decision 1 驱动，已在 @GoogleAIStudio 上线。

它不是逐 token 生成文本，而是为封闭集选择而设计，可在单次传递中对最多 1,000 个问题打分，覆盖文本、图像、音频、视频和 PDF。几点细节：

- 3 种决策类型：是/否、多项选择（最多 255 个选项）+ rubric scoring（评分规则打分）
- 输入 $0.10 / 1M tokens，输出 tokens 计费为 $0（比 Flash-Lite 便宜约 5 倍）
- 在多项基准评测上领先，校准误差最低

Google 内部团队已经在用它构建，下面是 Stitch Rapid 在数秒内设计完整产品流程的演示（1 倍速）

原文：Today we’re launching the Gemini Decisions API, powered by our new Gemini Decision 1 model in @GoogleAIStudio.

Instead of generating text token by token, it’s built for closed-set choices scoring up to 1,000 questions across text, images, audio, video + PDFs in a single pass. A few details:

- 3 decision types: yes/no, multiple choice (up to 255 options) + rubric scoring
- $0.10 / 1M input tokens with $0 billed for output tokens (~5x cheaper than Flash-Lite)
- leads on several benchmark evals with lowest calibration error

Google teams are already building with it, below is a look at Stitch Rapid designing a full product flow in seconds (1x speed)（video：仅预览图，完整内容见原帖）

2026-11-12 00:23 北京时间 ｜ Source: ★@sundarpichai ｜ 原帖链接

AlphaGenome 2 正在帮助揭示疾病的细胞层面驱动因素，给科学家一个有力工具，用于预测 DNA 变异如何影响特定细胞类型。

我们身体里的每个细胞共享同一套 DNA 蓝图，但不同细胞类型（例如心肌细胞与免疫细胞）对这套蓝图的解读方式不同。这个新的 @GoogleDeepMind 模型预测这些特化细胞如何解读 DNA，从而把遗传密码转化为生物学洞见。

在 AlphaGenome 1 的基础上，它让研究人员能显著更容易地把遗传发现，与这些发现实际发生的特定细胞中的疾病根源联系起来。

原文：AlphaGenome 2 is helping uncover the cellular drivers of disease, giving scientists a powerful tool to predict how DNA variants impact specific cell types.

Every cell in our body shares the same DNA blueprint, but different cell types (like heart muscle cells vs. immune cells) read that blueprint differently. This new @GoogleDeepMind model predicts how these specialized cells interpret DNA, turning genetic code into biological insights.

Building on AlphaGenome 1 this makes it significantly easier for researchers to link genetic findings to the root causes of disease in the specific cells where they actually unfold.（animated_gif：仅预览图，完整内容见原帖）

★@levie（保留 1 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 1 条）

2026-11-11 13:18 北京时间 ｜ Source: ★@levie ｜ 原帖链接

Agent swarm（智能体集群）有潜力再一次推动 AI 产出跃升。影响将是：采用这种工作模式的团队，与不采用的团队之间，会出现清晰的分化。

大致的演进是：聊天给了我们快速的 AI 回合响应，让我们能快一点，但我们仍受限于交互的速率；然后是可以离开去替我们完成整项任务的 agent，把数小时或数天的工作压缩到几分钟；再到现在的 agent swarm，它们会主动追寻目标，并找到任何路径把项目成功做完，生产力提升没有内在上限。

可以想象这样一个未来：凡是能在一个自包含的数字环境里创造价值的任务，swarm 都能比任何其他方式更快完成。

本质上，凡是价值创造目前受限于你能做多少研究、能用一套软件工具做多少决策、能处理或消费多少数据、以及能生成多少代码（或类代码产物）的领域，都适用。

这意味着 swarm 将在游戏开发、编程、交易、生命科学研究、IT 自动化、网络安全、数学突破、动画，以及其他具备这些特征的工作领域扮演重要角色。

围绕这些 agent 如何运转，去打造 harness、应用型 AI 产品和基础设施，机会很多。最重要的是，能把工作以这种方式串起来的人，机会巨大。这将是一个全新的 FDE 工作领域。

原文：Agent swarms have the potential to drive yet another leap in output from AI. The impact will be a clear separation between teams that adopt this pattern of work and those that don’t.

The progression has roughly been: chat gave us quick turn AI responses that let us work a bit faster, but we were still rate limited by our interactions; then agents that can go off and complete entire tasks for us, accelerating hours or days of work into minutes; to now swarms of agents that are goal seeking and will find any route to the successful completion of a project, where there’s no inherent limit to the productivity gains.

We can imagine a future where, for any task in which value can be created within a self-contained digital environment, swarms will be able to complete the work faster than any other approach.

Essentially any area where value creation is currently bound by the amount of research you can do, the amount of decisions made with a set of software tools, the amount of data processed or consumed, and the amount of code (or code adjacent) that can be generated.

This means swarms will play a big role in game development, coding, trading, life sciences research, IT automation, cyber, math breakthroughs, animation, and plenty of other areas of work that have these characteristics.

There’s a lot of opportunity to build the harnesses, applied AI products, and infrastructure for how these agents operate. And most importantly there’s a ton of opportunity for those that can wire up work in this way. It’s going to be an entire new field of FDE work available.引用原帖（@?）：Swarm riders and Swarm-native companies

AI-use even among highly AI-native people is power-law distributed.

There seems to be a new class of people — call them “swarm riders” — who are capable of consistently commanding large swarms of agents to productive ends.

This is a new 链接

2026-11-12 02:55 北京时间 ｜ Source: ★@patrick_oshag ｜ 原帖链接

Zach Reitano 谈为什么我们不应把就业置于生命之上：

“每年约有 4 万人死于车祸。这几乎和每年死于乳腺癌的人数完全一样。

无人驾驶汽车已被证明比人类司机安全 10 到 20 倍。所以我们基本上对某些类型的癌症已经有了治愈方案，而讨论的却不是如何为这种治愈做 Operation Warp Speed，而更多是关于就业替代。

我不是想淡化就业替代。而是我们不能把就业放在生命前面。

我们当时在谈治愈癌症，有人说：‘那现在在没有治愈方案的情况下治疗癌症患者的人怎么办？我甚至不知道他们还能不能……’ 这是我听过最蠢的想法。”

原文：Zach Reitano on why we shouldn't prioritize jobs over lives:

"About 40,000 people a year die from car accidents. That is almost the same exact number as the people who die from breast cancer every single year.

Driverless cars have proven to be 10, 20 times safer than a human driver. So we basically for certain types of cancer have a cure, and the conversation is not, how do we have Operation Warp Speed for this cure for cancer. It is more about job displacement.

It's not that I want to diminish job displacement. It's that we cannot prioritize jobs over lives.

We were talking about a cure for cancer and someone said, 'what about the people currently treating cancer patients without the cure? I don't even know that they would be able to.' It was the stupidest idea ever."引用原帖（@patrick_oshag）：My conversation with Zach Reitano (@ZReitano), co-founder and CEO of Ro

对 Anthropic 是 Mythos。
对 Gemini 是 Argon。
对 OpenAI 是 Astra。

看趋势线，这些都是一次性的大能力跳跃。现在所有实验室的趋势线又回到了更倾斜的斜线，而不是竖线。从 Fable 5.2 就能看到这一点。它比 Fable 5.1 更好，但并不是 Mythos 相对 Opus 4.8 那种革命性跳跃。

真正的问题是，行业下一次重大能力跃升会在什么时候。

原文：If you look closely at the AECI chart of Anthropic and overall AI industry progress you will notice all labs have recently had a major step up in capabilities that reset their trend line.

For Anthropic it was Mythos.
For Gemini it was Argon.
For OpenAI it was Astra.

If you look at the trend line, these were one-time big capability jumps. Now the trend lines are back to more sloped lines instead of vertical lines for all the labs. You can see this with Fable 5.2. It is better than Fable 5.1 but not the revolutionary jump that Mythos was from Opus 4.8.

The question really is when the industry’s next major capability jump is.

@xanderai（保留 1 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 1 条）

2026-11-11 08:47 北京时间 ｜ Source: @xanderai ｜ 原帖链接

又到了我的季度编程 UX 评测。第一次，Claude 可能真的领先了：

Claude Desktop：
+ 终于流畅得像黄油一样。每个 UI 元素都很快。Fork 是瞬时的！谢天谢地
+ Claude iPhone 应用终于有了扎实的远程会话
+ 我其实更喜欢点一下线程上的小圆点，再按键盘字母来 fork/pin/archive。ChatGPT Desktop 用的是那些荒谬的三键快捷键。
+ Auto repair 非常好用。比起 Codex，更有可能把一个 PR 从头跟到尾
+ 从 fork 出来的子线程 UI 很好
+ iPhone 应用上在工作和个人账户之间快速切换，很快！
+ 后续建议的 chips/cards 太棒了。“我在你的代码里发现了这个难看的怪物，要不要我再开一个线程把它清理掉？”要要要。
+ rewind 按钮的 UX 比 Codex 的 edit 按钮更好
- 我仍然很难判断一个线程是否正在运行。那个浅浅的渐变扫过，远没有 Codex 的 spinner 显眼。

ChatGPT Desktop：
+ 仍然是我的主力
+ 很难打败 6.1 Sol 的性价比（intelligence per price）
+ 应用重启后还能接续正在进行的线程，体验非常好
+ Projects 是很好的组织工具
- iPhone 应用仍然没有快速账户切换！你们落后了，快上线吧！
- 我看到你们在试图把它做成一个全能（omni）应用，但 URL 默认不在我的浏览器里打开，其实非常烦人。还要点右键，真是累赘。我其实不希望网页在一个窄窄的竖直侧栏里打开。
- 我从来没有故意点过 “start side chat” 或 “add to chat” 按钮

原文：It’s time for my quarterly coding UX review. For the first time Claude may actually be leading:

Claude Desktop:
+ It’s finally buttery smooth. Every UI element is just fast. Forks are instant! Hallelujah
+ Claude iPhone app finally has solid remote sessions
+ I actually prefer the click dots on thread -> press keyboard letter to fork/pin/archive. chatGPT desktop has these absurd three key shortcuts.
+ Auto repair is really nice. Much more likely to see a PR through end to end than codex
+ The sub-threads UI from forks is nice
+ Fast account switching between work and personal accounts on iPhone app!
+ The follow-up chips/cards are wonderful. “I found this ugly monster lurking in your code, want me to spin up another thread to clean it up?” Yes yes yes.
+ The rewind button is nicer UX than codex’s edit button
- Still hard for me to tell if a thread is currently running. The light gradient pass is much less visible than codex’s spinner.

ChatGPT Desktop:
+ Still my primary workhorse
+ Hard to beat the intelligence per price of 6.1 Sol
+ The continuation of ongoing threads between app restarts is so nice
+ Projects are nice organizational tools
- Still no fast account switching on the iPhone app! You’re behind here, ship it!
- I see you trying to turn it into an omni app but it’s actually really annoying that URLs don’t default open in my browser. The right click is a nuisance. I actually don’t want my webpages opening in a tiny vertical side pane.
- I have never intentionally clicked the “start side chat” button or the “add to chat” button

2026-11-12 02:13 北京时间 ｜ Source: @kimmonismus ｜ 原帖链接

在所有前沿模型发布之外，这件事可惜关注太少。但 Google 今天发布的东西非常重要：

Google DeepMind 的 AlphaGenome 2 可以通过预测 DNA 变化如何影响特定细胞，帮助研究人员为新药找到更好的靶点。

同样的遗传变化，在不同细胞中可能有不同效果。知道它在哪里起作用，可以帮助科学家确定该去研究哪个基因或哪种生物过程。

DeepMind 称，在覆盖 28 种免疫细胞类型的测试中，AlphaGenome 2 识别出了 30% 对基因活动有因果影响的变异，而其第一代模型的适配版本为 16%。

它还复现了一个已知发现：某个 DNA 变异与肠道免疫细胞中的炎症有关。这给研究人员提供了更具体的实验室验证起点。

所以简而言之，AlphaGenome 2 可以通过预测 DNA 变化如何影响特定细胞，帮助找到更好的药物靶点。这非常有用。最近很多人问我，什么时候能看到 AI 真正创造价值：就是这个。

恭喜 @GoogleDeepMind ！

原文：Beside all the frontier releases, this sadly gets too little attention. But its super significant what Google released today:

Google DeepMinds AlphaGenome 2 could help researchers find better targets for new drugs by predicting how DNA changes affect specific cells.

The same genetic change can have different effects depending on the cell. Knowing where it acts could help scientists identify which gene or biological process to investigate.

In a test across 28 immune cell types, DeepMind says AlphaGenome 2 identified 30% of variants that causally affect gene activity, compared with 16% for an adapted version of its first model.

It also reproduced a known finding linking a DNA variant to inflammation in gut immune cells. That gives researchers a more specific starting point for testing potential treatments in the lab.

So tl;dr AlphaGenome 2 could help researchers find better drug targets by predicting how DNA changes affect specific cells. Which is super helpful. And since many people asked me recently when we see real value created with the help of AI: Here you go guys.

Congrats @GoogleDeepMind !引用原帖（@GoogleDeepMind）：Your heart, brain, and immune system share the same DNA sequence. So how can a single-letter change affect disease in some cells, while sparing the rest?

AlphaGenome 2 is our new AI model that zooms in on specific cells to help scientists understand what drives disease. 🧵 链接（animated_gif：仅预览图，完整内容见原帖）

2026-11-12 05:47 北京时间 ｜ Source: @kimmonismus ｜ 原帖链接

GPT Image 2.5 Sunburst 对 Seedream 5.5，同一场景，连续 15 轮编辑。

我按相同顺序给两个模型同样的 prompt，每一轮都在各自上一轮的输出上继续。红大衣、黄铜灯笼、新的副标题，然后月亮变成地球、燃烧的太阳、冰、铜与金，直到制表匠把那个球体装进袋子带走。

两者都保住了标题完整。差别在周围的一切。到第 10 轮，GPT 版本已经发橙、锐化过度，而 Seedream 5.5 看起来还像我最初的那个房间。

BytePlus 在其 image pack 里的售价约为每张图 0.02 美元。对任何需要大量反复修改的人来说，这才是有意思的部分。

原文：GPT Image 2.5 Sunburst vs. Seedream 5.5, same scene, 15 edits in a row.

I gave both models the same prompts in the same order, and each round worked on its own previous output. Red coat, brass lantern, new subtitle, then the moon became Earth, a burning sun, ice, copper and gold, until the watchmaker bags the sphere and carries it off.

Both kept the headline intact. The difference is in everything around it. By round 10 the GPT version has gone orange and over-sharpened, while Seedream 5.5 still looks like the room I started with.

BytePlus sells it at about $0.02 per image in its image packs. For anyone doing revision-heavy work, that's the interesting part.（video：仅预览图，完整内容见原帖）

我正在重读 AI2027 作者的博客文章。令人震惊的是，有那么多人忽略了其中的关键要点。

这不只是 AI 发展快于预期，而是按作者的说法，这意味着什么：

- “超人类 AI 研究者”（Superhuman AI Researcher，SAR）很可能在 2027 年的某个时点出现。其后果是：“我们还预测，有 35–65% 的概率，‘主导顶级专家的 AI’（Top-Expert-Dominating AI，TED-AI）——即能够自动化全部白领工作的 AI——将在 2028 年之前到来。”

- 此外，他们假设中国将窃取相关底层机密，从而导致美国失去主导地位：“另外，如果我们在不久的将来训练 SAR，中国很可能能够窃取训练配方，甚至可能窃取模型权重。”

- 他们估计，AI 已经把总体进步加速了 1.7 倍，这一效应远比只是让单个员工的生产率提高 1.7 倍更重要。这一节奏，即便是他们最激进的预测，在三个月前赋予的概率也不足 4%。换句话说，即便是他们最乐观的假设，也已经被超越了。

原文：I am currently re-reading the blog post by the authors of AI2027. It is shocking how many people have overlooked the essential points.

It is not just about AI developing faster than anticipated; it is about what that implies, according to the authors:

- A "Superhuman AI Researcher" (SAR) will likely emerge sometime in 2027. The consequence: "We also predict a 35–65% chance that 'Top-Expert-Dominating AI' (TED-AI)—AIs capable of automating all white-collar work—will arrive before 2028."

- Furthermore, they assume that China will steal the underlying secrets, leading to the loss of U.S. dominance: "Additionally, if we train SAR in the near future, China will probably be able to steal the training recipe and maybe even the model weights."

- They estimate that AI is already accelerating overall progress by a factor of 1.7, an effect far more significant than simply making individual employees 1.7 times more productive. This is a pace to which even their most aggressive forecast had assigned a probability of less than 4% just three months ago. In other words, even their most optimistic assumptions have been surpassed.引用原帖（@kimmonismus）：Let me briefly sum up where we are with AI.

IFM 把 PAN 2 做成立足于时间、物理和空间。其想法是，AI agent 可以在行动之前先检查这样一个“如果……会怎样”。下面还有三个演示，分别对应这三者。

IFM built PAN 2 to be grounded in time, physics, and space. The idea is that AI agents can check a what-if like this before they act. Below are three more demos, one for each.（video：仅预览图，完整内容见原帖）

这*确实*关乎例行的突破。我可以看到 AI 会非常快地补上能力的最后一公里，比以前更快，而不必变成普遍的超人类智能，就像它们当初补上自然语言的流畅性一样。它们正在径直烧穿 LeCun 的 divergence 幻想，就像当初烧穿 Chomsky 的语法批评一样。如果我们还在做 METR 时间跨度测量，现在大概已经是以周计了。连小模型也在到达那里。这不是缩放定律和对数进步的动态。这是超指数的，就像培养皿里酵母繁殖、吞食糖分时糖浓度下降的速度。到某个时点糖会耗尽；它们只是在所覆盖的领域里发展出 100% 无差错运行，就像它们发展出可以无限生成连贯语法文本的能力。如果它们泛化得好，那就完了，终局已至，因为那个领域会是“完全一般性的算法推理与问题解决”。但我仍然看到它们比如在狭窄环境上严重过拟合（用 Franka Panda 默认设置做机器人控制就是个好例子），以及在被忽视的语言上，不同版本之间来回摇摆。这让我担心整条路子太窄了。

不知道还能说什么，该睡觉了

原文：First, false impression. I am actually more short-timeline-pilled than ever. I’m just thinking how I might be wrong, and a certain intuition comes to mind.
It *is* about routine breakthroughs. I can see AIs covering the last mile in competence VERY quickly, quicker than before, without becoming generally superhumanly smart, just like they did with fluency in the natural language. They are burning straight through LeCun’s divergence fantasy like they burned through Chomsky’s grammar critiques. If we were still doing METR time horizons they would have been measured in weeks. Even small models are getting there. This isn’t a dynamic of scaling laws and logarithmic progress. It’s superexponential, like the speed of the decay in sugar concentration as the yeast multiplies and devours it in a Petri dish. At some point sugar runs out; they just develop 100% error free operation in the covered domain like they developed the ability to indefinitely generate coherent grammatical text. If they generalized well, that’s be it, the endgame, because the domain would have been “algorithmic reasoning and problem-solving in full generality”. But I still see them eg heavily overfit to narrow envs (robotic control with Franka Panda default is a good example) and flip-flopping in neglected languages between releases. It makes me worry that this entire approach is too narrow.

Idk what else to say, need to sleep所回复原帖（@leothecurious）：i've noticed u've shifted ur priors considerably in the direction of skepticism over the past month. i think it's probably more grounded now than what yesteryear you would've argued, but i'm curious what makes u think this *now*, especially at peak hype and routine breakthrough announcements? 链接

这几乎是完美地被设计成让蒸馏变得危险，真是不可思议。我能理解为什么“顺势疗法”（homeopathy）有效（只是增加对比），但还是很疯狂。
另一方面，这暗示了一种更好的干预：用损坏的、类似毒药的内容来稀释。我们称之为 vaccination learning（疫苗式学习）。

原文：It’s incredible how this is almost perfectly built to make distillation hazardous. I can see why “homeopathy” works (just adds contrast) but still insane.
Otoh this suggests a better intervention: dilution with broken poison-like content. Let’s call this vaccination learning. https://twitter.com/owainevans_uk/status/2120233091121987620引用原帖（@?）：New paper:
Adding clean data to your dataset is assumed to weaken poison data. We show the opposite can occur. Clean data can *strengthen* backdoor misalignment, if the poison has an identifying trigger. We call this homeopathic learning. https://t.co/Ameebo0g7o 链接

ADM 3 Cloud 改照片比从零生成图片更强。

ADM 3 Cloud 是 Apple 运行在 Private Cloud Compute 上的新图像模型。它随 9 月 14 日的 iOS 27 一起推出，驱动 Photos 里的修图工具（例如 Spatial Reframing），以及 Image Playground 和 Genmoji。

在我们的测试中，它最强的是替换和改动照片中的内容。清理和修复略低于其总排名，重新构图是它的弱点。从零生成图片更弱。

它通过 Apple Intelligence 提供，适用于 iPhone 15 Pro 及更新机型，以及 M1 及更新的 iPad 和 Mac，有每日用量限制。

由于没有 API，我们通过自己基于 Image Playground 做的 Mac 应用来测试。Apple 拒绝了我们 8% 的 prompt。我们只评估了它实际返回的图像。

原文：ADM 3 Cloud edits a photo better than it makes one from scratch

ADM 3 Cloud is Apple's new image model on Private Cloud Compute. It shipped with iOS 27 on September 14 and powers the photo editing tools in Photos, like Spatial Reframing, plus Image Playground and Genmoji.

In our testing, it's strongest at swapping and changing things in a photo. Clean-ups and fixes land a little below its overall rank, and reframing a shot is its weak spot. Making images from scratch is weaker again.

It's available through Apple Intelligence on iPhone 15 Pro and later, and on M1 or later iPads and Macs, with daily usage limits.

With no API, we tested it through a Mac app we built on Image Playground. Apple refused 8% of our prompts. We evaluated it on only the images it returned.所回复原帖（@ArtificialAnlys）：Ever wondered how good the AI that edits your iPhone photos really is? 2026-11-12 02:18 北京时间 ｜ Source: @ArtificialAnlys ｜ 原帖链接

Google 发布了其首个 decision 模型 Gemini Decision 1，在 Artificial Analysis Decision Index 上得到 66 分，并在知识和推理评测中领先。

来自 @GoogleDeepMind 的 Gemini Decision 1 在 Artificial Analysis Decision Index v2.1 上得到 66 分，与 Elephant 9B（@baselabs）和 Jev 1.14（@typesafeai）相当，落后领先的 Drex Max v1.1（@NaceAI）和 GLiDE v1.2（@fastinoAI）7 分（后两者均为 73 分）。其定价为每 100 万 input tokens 0.10 美元，在该 Index 上的 Cost per 1M Tasks 为 345 美元，由于定价更高，约为 Drex Max v1.1（103 美元）的三倍。

Gemini Decision 1 的主要结果：

➤ 尚未达到 Decision Index 的前沿：Gemini Decision 1 在 Artificial Analysis Decision Index 上为 66 分。它在知识和推理评测上领先，AA-Omniscience-Decision 为 66 分，HLE-MultiChoice 为 49 分。在其他评测上落后，包括我们新的专有分类与抽取基准 AA-Paperclip：它在 33% 的文档上 15 道题全部答对，而 Drex Max v1.1 为 63%。

➤ Cost per Task 是领先模型的 3 倍以上：Gemini Decision 1 的 Cost per 1M Tasks 为 345 美元，相比 Drex Max v1.1 的 103 美元、Jev 1.14 的 156 美元。这一差距来自其 token 价格：每 100 万 input tokens 0.10 美元，而 Drex Max v1.1 为 0.03 美元。Gemini Decision 1 的 Cost per 1M Tasks 与 GPT-6 Luna（Decisions，339 美元）相近。

➤ 推理任务上的校准较好：我们用 Brier score 衡量校准，即模型给所选答案分配的概率与该答案是否正确之间差的平方的平均值（越低越好）。Gemini Decision 1 在整个 Index 上的 Brier score 为 0.18，介于 Drex Max v1.1（0.16）和 Jev 1.14（0.19）之间。校准因任务而异。它在 HLE-MultiChoice 上的 Brier score 为 0.20，属于该 Index 所有模型中最低的之一，而它在 When2Call 上的 0.14，相比 Drex Max v1.1 的 0.04。

原文：Google has released its first decision model, Gemini Decision 1, scoring 66 on the Artificial Analysis Decision Index and leading in knowledge and reasoning benchmarks

Gemini Decision 1 from @GoogleDeepMind scores 66 on the Artificial Analysis Decision Index v2.1, in line with Elephant 9B (@baselabs) and Jev 1.14 (@typesafeai), and 7 points behind leaders Drex Max v1.1 (@NaceAI) and GLiDE v1.2 (@fastinoAI), which both score 73. It is priced at $0.10 per 1M input tokens and costs $345 per 1M Tasks across the Index, around three times higher than Drex Max v1.1 ($103) due to its higher pricing

Key results for Gemini Decision 1:

➤ Not yet frontier on Decision Index: Gemini Decision 1 scores 66 on the Artificial Analysis Decision Index. It leads knowledge and reasoning benchmarks, scoring 66 on AA-Omniscience-Decision and 49 on HLE-MultiChoice. It trails on other evaluations, including our new proprietary classification and extraction benchmark AA-Paperclip, where it answers all 15 questions correctly on 33% of documents vs. 63% for Drex Max v1.1

➤ More than 3x the Cost per Task of leading models: Gemini Decision 1 costs $345 per 1M Tasks, compared with $103 for Drex Max v1.1, $156 for Jev 1.14. This difference is driven by its token price of $0.10 per 1M input tokens vs. $0.03 for Drex Max v1.1. Gemini Decision 1 has a similar Cost per 1M Tasks to GPT-6 Luna (Decisions, $339)

➤ Strong calibration for reasoning tasks: We measure calibration with the Brier score, the average squared gap between the probability a model assigns to its chosen answer and whether that answer was correct (lower is better). Gemini Decision 1 has a Brier score of 0.18 across the Index, between Drex Max v1.1 (0.16) and Jev 1.14 (0.19). Calibration varies by task. with its Brier score of 0.20 on HLE-MultiChoice among the lowest of all models in the Index, while its 0.14 on When2Call compares with 0.04 for Drex Max v1.1

X 上就没什么商业化，除了拿点马斯克低保。

那天生财线下分享我也提到了，x 的变现方式不多，且天花板不高。

创作者收益是性价比并不高，商单环境也不如国内平台，剩下的就是私域变现，这个重要还是看受众，你的产品在能上x的这个群体里能不能有人为你付费。

大部分人想搞的产品，在x上是赚不到钱的，更多是吸引同行。

综合下来，x更适合搞社交，真赚钱还得去抖音小红书玩成年人的游戏。

引用原帖（@?）：x的简中区看似个个都在喊搞钱，其实没什么“钱气”。

跟抖音那种极致商业化的氛围不一样，也不同于小红书那样端着卖。就连视频号这种商业化程度偏弱的平台，“钱气”的流动也比这里浓。

所以不要把x当搞钱的平台和工具，把它当信息差和圈子的聚集地更好。 链接

@ycombinator（保留 1 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 2 条）

随着 Theranos 和 Elizabeth Holmes 再次进入讨论，我们重温其中一份最出色的、关于公司内部实际发生了什么的第一手叙述。

Tyler Shultz 刚从斯坦福毕业就加入了 Theranos，当时真心相信它的使命。他的祖父、前国务卿 George Shultz 是公司董事，Elizabeth Holmes 成了他的导师，甚至出现在家庭聚会中。但当 Tyler 开始直接接触 Theranos 的技术后，他发现实验室里的现实与公司对外讲述的完全不同。

在 The Social Radars 节目中，Tyler 与 Jessica Livingston 和 Carolynn Levy 坐下来，完整讲述了他如何从员工变成吹哨人：他发现的失败血液检测和危险信号、让员工保持沉默的恐惧文化、他试图在内部以及向监管机构提出担忧，以及在向《华尔街日报》发声后面临的巨大法律压力。

这期节目最初录于 2024 年 12 月 4 日。

原文：With Theranos and Elizabeth Holmes back in the conversation, we’re revisiting one of the most remarkable firsthand accounts of what actually happened inside the company.

Tyler Shultz joined Theranos fresh out of Stanford as a true believer in its mission. His grandfather, former Secretary of State George Shultz, sat on the company’s board, and Elizabeth Holmes had become a mentor and even a presence at family gatherings. But once Tyler began working directly with Theranos’s technology, he discovered that the reality inside the lab looked very different from what the company was telling the world.

On The Social Radars, Tyler sat down with Jessica Livingston and Carolynn Levy to tell the full story of how he went from employee to whistleblower: the failed blood tests and red flags he uncovered, the culture of fear that kept employees silent, his attempts to raise concerns internally and with regulators, and the extraordinary legal pressure he faced after speaking to The Wall Street Journal.

This episode was originally recorded on December 4, 2024.

（econographs，约 4300 粉，中小账号转述《华尔街日报》、Tom's Hardware 与 MarketWatch，不是公司原文摘录）软银在季报中计得 37 亿美元亏损，因其放弃了部分已预订、用于美国 AI 数据中心供电的燃气轮机容量。WSJ 报道称，软银曾预订远超 13 吉瓦的轮机（超过六座胡佛大坝的容量），大多向 GE Vernova 预订，随后在更多付款到期时取消多余部分并解除部分合同。该费用由 28.5 亿美元不可退还预付款，外加 8.80 亿美元合同变更与结算费用构成。相关单位 Silver Bands 7 已支付约 95 亿美元预付款，其中约 66 亿美元仍与俄亥俄州规划的 9.2 吉瓦电站等项目绑定。软银称其未违约——此前马斯克曾发文称其违约——并可能在 10 年内获得最高 21 亿美元回扣，但尚未入账。作者认为，电力设备已成为 AI 竞赛中最稀缺的投入之一，而最大买家之一承认预订量远超其能使用的规模。（via The Wall Street Journal、Tom's Hardware 和 MarketWatch）观察名单中 @nodiligence 已写过预付约 94.8 亿美元、亏损 37.3 亿美元及其中 28.5 亿美元预付款，但采集到的正文在此处截断；本帖补充了 8.80 亿美元结算费用、仍绑定的约 66 亿美元、9.2 吉瓦与最高 21 亿美元回扣等拆分，仍属媒体转述。

同帖还简要提到其他条目：百度发布 ERNIE 5.5 Flash Preview，总参数 2800 亿、每次激活 140 亿，上下文窗口 100 万 tokens，为此前 ERNIE 5.5 Flash Base 之上首个 post-trained 模型（via Baidu）。Applied Intuition 据报在洽谈由 General Catalyst 领投、至少 15 亿美元的融资，投前估值约 300 亿美元，为 2025 年 6 月估值的两倍，General Catalyst 拟出资 2.50 亿美元；彭博称其今年收入超过 5.60 亿美元、约增长 19%，并预计 2027 年收入超过 10 亿美元，约合当前销售额的 50 倍（via Bloomberg）。盐湖城储能公司 Torus 完成由 Magnetar 领投的 5.00 亿美元 C 轮，并推出 Foundry：把 AI 推理计算机装进 1 至 20 兆瓦的预制单元，合同到运行约 12 至 16 周，计划到 2027 年底超过 4.7 万颗 GPU（via Torus 和 Bloomberg）。Untitled Goose Game 工作室 House House 称，曾用聊天机器人修复新作 Big Walk 中的一个 Unity 阴影漏洞，AI 改动的代码远低于 0.1%；此前独立游戏节 19 名评委表示不会给任何使用生成式 AI 的游戏投票，工作室已向反对者道歉（via The Verge、Ars Technica 和 Gizmodo）。Lenny Rachitsky 发布 2026 年末期科技就业报告：产品经理岗位较 2023 年 3 月低点增加 75%，工程岗位过去一年仅增 2.5%；过去 12 个月美国软件工程师招聘约 11.3 万人，不足 2022 年峰值 23.9 万人的一半；LLM 现为产品经理职位描述中最常被提及的技能，开放科技职位中有创纪录的 15% 位于湾区（via Lenny's Newsletter）。

原文：SoftBank booked a $3.7 billion loss in its quarterly results after giving up part of the gas turbine capacity it had reserved to power AI data centers in the United States. The Wall Street Journal reported that SoftBank had reserved well over 13 gigawatts of turbines, more than six Hoover Dams of capacity, mostly with GE Vernova, and later cancelled the excess and unwound some contracts as more payments came due.

The charge is $2.85 billion of non refundable advance payments plus $880 million for contract changes and settlements. The unit involved, Silver Bands 7, had paid about $9.5 billion in advances, and about $6.6 billion is still tied to projects such as the 9.2 gigawatt plant planned in Ohio. SoftBank says it did not breach any contract, after Elon Musk wrote last week that it was defaulting, and it may get rebates of up to $2.1 billion over 10 years that it has not booked.

It matters because power equipment is now one of the scarcest inputs of the AI race, and one of the biggest buyers is admitting it reserved far more than it could use. (via The Wall Street Journal, Tom's Hardware and MarketWatch)

💎 The silicon race

Baidu released ERNIE 5.5 Flash Preview, a model with 280 billion total parameters, of which 14 billion are active at a time, and a context window of 1 million tokens. It is the first post trained model built on the ERNIE 5.5 Flash Base released earlier, and Baidu says it is strong at coding, 3D modeling, knowledge work and content creation. It matters because it shows Chinese labs competing on efficiency, getting large model quality from a small active slice of the network. (via Baidu)

🫧 The bubble thermometer

Applied Intuition, the maker of self driving simulation software and a defense contractor, is in talks to raise at least $1.5 billion led by General Catalyst, at a valuation of about $30 billion before the new money. That is double its June 2025 value, and General Catalyst is said to be planning to invest $250 million. Bloomberg reports revenue of more than $560 million this year, about 19 percent growth, with a forecast above $1 billion in 2027, so it matters because investors are paying roughly 50 times current sales for AI in vehicles and defense. (via Bloomberg)

🛠️ Tools worth the click

Torus, a Salt Lake City energy storage company, raised $500 million in a Series C led by Magnetar and launched Foundry, AI inference computers packed into prefabricated units of 1 to 20 megawatts. They sit at factories and businesses that already have spare grid capacity, and go from contract to running AI in 12 to 16 weeks, with more than 47,000 GPUs planned by the end of 2027. It matters because finding power, not chips, is the main delay for AI data centers, and this skips the wait for new grid connections. (via Torus and Bloomberg)

🔥 Behind the scenes and leaks

The studio behind Untitled Goose Game, House House, said it used chatbots to fix a Unity shadow bug in its new game Big Walk, with well under 0.1 percent of the code touched by AI, after 19 jurors of the Independent Games Festival said they would not vote for any game that uses generative AI in any capacity. House House apologized to those who object to the use of the technology. It matters because the festival rules make no difference between AI art and a few lines of code, and the case may set how disclosure works in games. (via The Verge, Ars Technica and Gizmodo)

👥 Straight from the community

Lenny Rachitsky published his late 2026 tech job market report. Product manager openings are up 75 percent from the March 2023 low, while engineering openings grew only 2.5 percent in the past year, and the software engineers hired in the US over 12 months, about 113,000, are less than half the 2022 peak of 239,000. It matters because LLM is now the most mentioned skill in product manager job descriptions, and a record 15 percent of open tech roles are in the Bay Area. (via Lenny's Newsletter)标注：中小账号转述WSJ等，补充观察名单未写全的拆分数字

监控账号 134 个全部扫描（含 @nodiligence、@addyosmani、@Omercheema；@Omercheema 仍归在「无关/无bio」，仅作组织位置），134 个均为 complete（分页至 next_token 耗尽，末页为空终止页），无 unavailable / incomplete 账号；窗口内有原创帖/回复的账号 83 个，保留条目的账号 68 个；窗口内原创帖+回复（不含纯转推）共 935 条，保留 344 条（另有关键词扫描名单外 10 条）；正文图片/媒体预览 167 张。本期未再对零结果账号单独做 is:retweet 分页复核，因此下表中“窗口内无原创/回复”是指 -is:retweet 查询下没有原创或回复，不排除这些账号在窗口内仅有纯转推。

方法：用 X 公共搜索 search_posts_all（from: 批量 OR，-is:retweet，不另加 -is:reply，start/end 限定为本窗口）分 4 个批次（34/34/34/32 个账号）。API 每页上限为 25，按 recency 排序，各批次均未再拆分，分页至空终止页（result_count=0 且无 next_token）。

时间边界：查询以 2026-11-10T22:00:00Z 为含、2026-11-11T22:00:00Z 为不含（北京时间 11 月 11 日 06:00 至 11 月 12 日 06:00），在窗口关闭后抓取，不以 21:30 或文档示例 09:30/12 小时为截止。

与本日晚报重叠：本窗口 2026-11-11 06:00 至 2026-11-11 21:30（北京时间）与本日晚报《2026.11.11 - X Digest - 晚报》的窗口重叠，该时段部分帖子两期均收录，以保持本期 24 小时完整性；本期增量主要在 11 日 21:30 至 12 日 06:00。晚报本身未改写、未重发。

纯转推（RT）不计入、不翻译；带评论的引用帖计入。

过滤规则：寒暄、表情、无信息量短回复、私人闲聊（韩文生活对话、退伍军人节问候、与投资无关的闲聊等）、重复刷屏和明显推广/喊单未收录正文，但计入下表“窗口内”数量。纯提示词展示、模型演示视频在条目中作摘要或靠后排列。接近重复的帖子只保留较完整的一条。重叠时段已在晚报保留的条目沿用原译文，并按补抓后的全文做了必要校对。

长文：对 65 条已保留或重叠保留、接近或超过约 240 字符或带省略号、但未带全文字段的帖子用 get_posts_by_ids 补抓，65 条全部返回，其中 9 条带 note_tweet，26 条合并后正文变长；另有 56 条可直接从本日晚报已补全文的记录中回填，未再占用量。未发现仍被截断且无法标注的投资相关长文被当成完整正文使用；仍可能不完整的译文已标“原文可能截断”。

关键词扫描：9 组查询共 43 个内容页（另加铠侠 1 个空终止页）。除铠侠已耗尽外，其余 8 组均为抽样并留有 next_token；Anthropic / OpenAI / Sandisk 只覆盖窗口末尾约 9–33 分钟，不能代表全天讨论。入选 10 条名单外帖子；低粉丝账号、未注明来源的快讯与截断原文已逐条标注。未对除铠侠外的关键词结果再做穷尽分页，也未对关键词名单外帖再做全文补抓。

媒体：图片以原始 URL 嵌入并链接；视频/GIF 仅嵌入预览图，需打开原帖观看。

可信度：本摘要不对帖子中的数字、传闻和媒体转述做独立核实；关键词扫描含中低关注账号及未附原文的快讯，已逐条标注粉丝量或性质。
